<span class="line"></span></code></pre></div></div></div></span><p>Images are returned as URLs by default. URLs are temporary, so download or process promptly. You can also request <a href="#base64-output" class="text-primary underline has-[code]:decoration-solar has-[code]:decoration-dotted has-[code]:underline-offset-2">base64 output</a> for embedding images directly.</p><hr/><span class="not-prose"><h2 id="configuration" class="scroll-mt-28 transition-colors duration-100 ease-in-out lg:scroll-mt-24 text-primary font-display text-balance text-2xl leading-tight tracking-tight sm:text-3xl mb-4 mt-12"><a class="not-prose" href="#configuration">Configuration</a></h2></span><span class="not-prose"><h3 id="multiple-images" class="scroll-mt-28 transition-colors duration-100 ease-in-out lg:scroll-mt-24 text-primary font-display text-xl leading-snug tracking-tight sm:text-2xl mb-3 mt-10"><a class="not-prose" href="#multiple-images">Multiple Images</a></h3></span><p>Generate multiple images in a single request with the <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">n</code></span> parameter (<span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">1</code></span>–<span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">10</code></span>). On the REST API and OpenAI-compatible SDKs, <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">n</code></span> is optional and defaults to <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">1</code></span>. The xAI Python SDK uses <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">sample()</code></span> for a single image and <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">sample_batch(n=...)</code></span> for more than one — <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">n</code></span> is required on <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">sample_batch()</code></span>.</p><span class="not-prose"><div class="relative flex h-full flex-col rounded-xl bg-l1 text-fg border <span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -d</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> '{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    "model": "grok-imagine-image-2.0",</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    "prompt": "A futuristic city skyline at night",</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  }'</span></span>
<span class="line"></span></code></pre></div></div></div></span><span class="not-prose"><h3 id="aspect-ratio" class="scroll-mt-28 transition-colors duration-100 ease-in-out lg:scroll-mt-24 text-primary font-display text-xl leading-snug tracking-tight sm:text-2xl mb-3 mt-10"><a class="not-prose" href="#aspect-ratio">Aspect Ratio</a></h3></span><p>Control image dimensions with the <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">aspect_ratio</code></span> parameter. When omitted, the default is <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">auto</code></span>, which lets the model pick the best ratio for the prompt.</p><div <span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    model: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"grok-imagine-image-2.0"</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    prompt: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"An astronaut performing EVA in LEO."</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    resolution: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"2k"</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">});</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(response.data[</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">].url);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span></code></pre></div><div class="w-full rounded-[inherit] hidden" data-language="javascriptAISDK"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { xai } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "@ai-sdk/xai"</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { generateImage } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "ai"</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">image</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> generateImage</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    model: xai.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">image</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"grok-imagine-image-2.0"</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    prompt: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"An astronaut performing EVA in LEO."</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    },</span></span>

<span class="line"></span></code></pre></div><div class="w-full rounded-[inherit] hidden" data-language="bash"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">curl</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> -X</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> POST</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> https://api.x.ai/v1/images/generations</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \</span></span>

<span class="line"></span></code></pre></div></div></div></span><span class="not-prose"><h3 id="quality" class="scroll-mt-28 transition-colors duration-100 ease-in-out lg:scroll-mt-24 text-primary font-display text-xl leading-snug tracking-tight sm:text-2xl mb-3 mt-10"><a class="not-prose" href="#quality">Quality</a></h3></span><p>Control generation quality with the optional <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">quality</code></span> parameter. Allowed values are <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">low</code></span>, <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">medium</code></span>, and <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">auto</code></span>. When omitted, the default is <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">auto</code></span>, which lets the service choose the quality for each request. Auto currently uses <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">low</code></span> for image generation and <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">medium</code></span> for <a href="/developers/model-capabilities/images/editing" class="text-primary underline has-[code]:decoration-solar has-[code]:decoration-dotted has-[code]:underline-offset-2">image editing</a>. Images are billed at the quality they are served at (see <a href="/developers/pricing" class="text-primary underline has-[code]:decoration-solar has-[code]:decoration-dotted has-[code]:underline-offset-2">Pricing</a>). Pass <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">low</code></span> or <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">medium</code></span> to pin a specific quality. The parameter is only supported for <span class="not-prose"><code class="font-mono text-[0.95em] text-solar">grok-imagine-image-2.0</code></span>.</p><span class="not-prose"><div class="relative flex
